# Conf42 blog

> Write-ups of talks from Conf42's free online tech conferences, conference roundups, sponsor keynotes and webinar recaps. Every post has a Markdown twin (markdown); every talk page too. Machine-readable: https://www.conf42.com/blog.json


## November 2026

CONF42 = 42% off.

- [Name it, scope it, gate it, record it: agents in a regulated enterprise](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2026/shipping-agentic-ai-in-a-regulated-enterprise.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2026): Shanker Valipireddy (Aflac) at Conf42 MLOps 2026 on a four-step control plane for agents: identity, scoped grant, policy gate and immutable audit.
- [Your agent's refund check trusts an order ID the model picked](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2026/self-signed-evidence-llm-agent-checks.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2026): Danila Redikultsev (Aeroclub) at Conf42 MLOps 2026 on self-signed evidence: code checks that pass because the LLM agent chose the value they read.
- [Agent reliability is a property of the platform around the model](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2026/self-healing-cloud-infrastructure-agentic-ai.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2026): Amit Anand at Conf42 MLOps 2026 on the platform agents need to heal infrastructure: durable runs, a policy gate, run IDs and autonomy per action class.

- [A loan valuation you cannot replay is one you cannot defend](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2026/reproducible-pipelines-illiquid-loan-valuation.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2026): Ankur Mehra at Conf42 MLOps 2026 on valuing illiquid loans with pinned versions, signed run manifests and event-driven revaluation.
- [Record, replay, fork: debugging an agent that failed mid-run](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2026/record-replay-fork-failed-agent-runs.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2026): Reza Amini (Flashback) at Conf42 MLOps 2026 on saving agent runs as cassettes, replaying them offline and forking a fix from the first wrong call.
- [Why logistic regression beat a CNN and an LSTM on noisy product names](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2026/logistic-regression-beats-neural-nets-product-names.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2026): Vladimir Beskorovainyi (Besk Tech) at Conf42 MLOps 2026 on character n-gram logistic regression beating a CNN, an LSTM and DistilBERT on receipt text.

It must be continuous, covering failures, load, and disasters. Delayed validation creates “resilience debt,” increasing risk. A holistic approach—combining chaos, load, and DR testing—plus cross-team collaboration and AI-driven insights improves reliability and reduces impact.

## September 2025
- [Conf42 MLOps 2025 roundup: where the lab meets production](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2025/mlops-2025-roundup-lab-meets-production.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2025): Conf42 MLOps 2025 in review, from warehouse robots and vendor lockouts to trillion-parameter training, AgentCore, zero trust pipelines and fintech ML.
- [Zero trust for ML pipelines, stage by stage](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2025/zero-trust-mlops-four-pipeline-stages.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2025): Sudheer Obbu of JPMorgan Chase at Conf42 MLOps 2025 on applying never-trust-always-verify to data, training, serving and monitoring in MLOps.
- [Warehouse AI that aces the lab can still fail on the floor](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2025/warehouse-ai-failures-lab-is-not-the-floor.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2025): Shubham Beldar (Symbotic) at Conf42 MLOps 2025 on four warehouse automation failure patterns and the MLOps habits that prevent them.
- [How a CNN-LSTM pipeline cut VoIP fault detection to minutes](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2025/voip-monitoring-mlops-cnn-lstm-oracle.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2025): Krishna Munnaluru (Oracle) at Conf42 MLOps 2025 on a Kafka, Kubeflow and MLflow pipeline that monitors carrier-grade VoIP with neural networks.
- [Put AI data governance inside the CI/CD pipeline](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2025/training-data-declarations-genai-governance.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2025): Bhanu Maryala (Informatica) at Conf42 MLOps 2025 on Training Data Declarations, a framework that runs compliance checks inside MLOps pipelines.
- [Spam detection at billion-user scale is an ensemble and a graph](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2025/spam-detection-mlops-at-billion-user-scale.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2025): Prabhakar Singh (Meta) at Conf42 MLOps 2025 on production MLOps for multi-modal spam detection, from smart sampling to graph-based campaign detection.
- [Before you repatriate, consider sky computing](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2025/sky-computing-instead-of-cloud-repatriation.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2025): Brian Irish (Ditto) at Conf42 MLOps 2025 on sky computing and SkyPilot, an intercloud broker approach to vendor lock-in, egress fees and GPU scarcity.
- [SiriusXM cut model deployments from 45 days to four hours](https://www.conf42.com/blog/mlops2025/siriusxm-mlops-deploys-45-days-to-4-hours.md) (Conf42 MLOps 2025): Jimmy Katiyar of SiriusXM at Conf42 MLOps 2025 on validation gates, CI/CD, monitoring and Kubernetes behind a jump from MLOps level one to three.
